364fa85681bee0cc7d9b1d82b3807a40ac07a789: $$u=\sum u_{i}e_{i}$$

541ecb0e0ec66089acad4d34834b9d97fb3727cd: $$\partial f(x)[u]=\sum_{i} \partial_{i}f(x)u_{i}= \langle \nabla f(x), u\rangle$$
